#@@@ FILE modules/tool_select/SelectConfig.php sha1=cfcaf5a49d5635d299c18e1a81b88d3c1d15b730 base=- mode=644 enc=text nl=1
<?php
/**
 * 讀取選機表設定：config/tool_select.ini（共用）+ config/tool_select/<機型>.ini（機型專屬）
 *
 *   ToolSelectConfig::shared()       共用設定（機型、超規底色、狀態底色）
 *   ToolSelectConfig::models()       機型代碼 => 顯示名稱
 *   ToolSelectConfig::forModel($m)   該機型的機台、分類、項目（含每台的有效 spec）
 *   ToolSelectConfig::judge($v, $s)  數值 + spec → '' | 'ooc' | 'oos'
 *
 * 每次請求只讀一次檔案。設定錯誤丟 ToolSelectConfigException，頁面會顯示訊息
 */
class ToolSelectConfigException extends RuntimeException
{
}

class ToolSelectConfig
{
    /** spec 四條的 key，順序 = 表格欄位順序 */
    public static $SPEC_KEYS = array('upper_oos', 'upper_ooc', 'lower_ooc', 'lower_oos');
    public static $COLOR_KEYS = array('ooc_bg', 'oos_bg', 'ooc_bg_light', 'oos_bg_light');

    private static $shared = null;
    private static $models = array();

    public static function shared()
    {
        if (self::$shared !== null) {
            return self::$shared;
        }
        $ini = self::parse(AISLE_ROOT . '/config/tool_select.ini', 'config/tool_select.ini');

        $models = array();
        foreach (array_get($ini, 'models', array()) as $k => $v) {
            $k = strtoupper(trim($k));
            if (preg_match('/^[A-Z0-9_]{1,16}$/', $k)) {
                $models[$k] = (string) $v;
            }
        }
        if (!$models) {
            throw new ToolSelectConfigException('config/tool_select.ini 的 [models] 沒有任何機型');
        }

        $defaults = array(
            'ooc_bg' => '#7A5A00', 'oos_bg' => '#8B0000', 'ooc_bg_light' => '#FFE08A', 'oos_bg_light' => '#FF9C9C',
        );
        $colorIni = array_get($ini, 'color', array());
        $colors = array();
        foreach ($defaults as $k => $d) {
            $colors[$k] = self::color(array_get($colorIni, $k, ''), $d);
        }

        $status = array();
        foreach (array_get($ini, 'status', array()) as $k => $v) {
            $c = self::color($v, '');
            $k = trim((string) $k);
            if ($k !== '' && $c !== '') {
                $status[strtolower($k)] = $c;
            }
        }

        self::$shared = array('models' => $models, 'colors' => $colors, 'status' => $status);
        return self::$shared;
    }

    public static function models()
    {
        $s = self::shared();
        return $s['models'];
    }

    /**
     * 該機型的設定
     * @return array array(
     *   'model', 'label', 'json_path', 'sample',
     *   'tools'      => array('APAN01', ...),
     *   'categories' => array(代號 => 名稱),
     *   'items'      => array(array('id','category','label','key','unit','decimals',
     *                               'spec' => array(4 個值或 null), 'toolSpec' => array(機台 => array(4)),
     *                               'colors' => array(覆蓋的顏色), 'href'), ...))  已依分類排序
     */
    public static function forModel($model)
    {
        if (isset(self::$models[$model])) {
            return self::$models[$model];
        }
        $shared = self::shared();
        if (!isset($shared['models'][$model])) {
            throw new ToolSelectConfigException('沒有這個機型：' . $model);
        }
        $rel = 'config/tool_select/' . $model . '.ini';
        $ini = self::parse(AISLE_ROOT . '/' . $rel, $rel);

        $page = array_get($ini, 'page', array());

        // 機台：大寫、去重複，保留順序
        $tools = array();
        foreach ((array) array_get(array_get($ini, 'tools', array()), 'tool', array()) as $t) {
            $t = strtoupper(trim((string) $t));
            if ($t !== '' && preg_match('/^[A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9_.\-]{0,63}$/', $t) && !in_array($t, $tools, true)) {
                $tools[] = $t;
            }
        }

        $categories = array();
        foreach (array_get($ini, 'category', array()) as $code => $label) {
            $code = trim((string) $code);
            if (!preg_match('/^[a-z0-9_]{1,32}$/', $code)) {
                throw new ToolSelectConfigException($rel . ' [category] 的代號「' . $code . '」只能用小寫英文、數字、底線');
            }
            $categories[$code] = (string) $label;
        }

        $items = array();
        $order = 0;
        foreach ($ini as $section => $v) {
            if (strpos($section, 'item.') !== 0 || !is_array($v)) {
                continue;
            }
            $id = substr($section, 5);
            $where = $rel . ' [' . $section . ']';
            if (!preg_match('/^[a-z0-9_]{1,64}$/', $id)) {
                throw new ToolSelectConfigException($where . '：項目代號只能用小寫英文、數字、底線');
            }
            $cat = trim((string) array_get($v, 'category', ''));
            if (!isset($categories[$cat])) {
                throw new ToolSelectConfigException($where . '：category「' . $cat . '」不在 [category] 裡');
            }

            $spec = array();
            foreach (self::$SPEC_KEYS as $k) {
                $spec[] = self::number(array_get($v, $k, ''), $where . ' ' . $k);
            }
            self::checkOrder($spec, $where);

            // 機台覆蓋：upper_oos.APAN03 = 6
            $toolSpec = array();
            foreach ($v as $k => $val) {
                if (strpos($k, '.') === false) {
                    continue;
                }
                list($base, $tool) = explode('.', $k, 2);
                $idx = array_search($base, self::$SPEC_KEYS, true);
                if ($idx === false) {
                    throw new ToolSelectConfigException($where . '：不認得的欄位 ' . $k . '（機台覆蓋只能用 upper_oos / upper_ooc / lower_ooc / lower_oos）');
                }
                $tool = strtoupper(trim($tool));
                if (!in_array($tool, $tools, true)) {
                    throw new ToolSelectConfigException($where . '：' . $k . ' 的機台 ' . $tool . ' 不在 [tools] 裡');
                }
                if (!isset($toolSpec[$tool])) {
                    $toolSpec[$tool] = $spec;
                }
                $toolSpec[$tool][$idx] = self::number($val, $where . ' ' . $k);
            }
            foreach ($toolSpec as $tool => $s) {
                self::checkOrder($s, $where . ' 機台 ' . $tool);
            }

            $colors = array();
            foreach (self::$COLOR_KEYS as $k) {
                if (array_key_exists($k, $v) && trim((string) $v[$k]) !== '') {
                    $c = self::color($v[$k], '');
                    if ($c === '') {
                        throw new ToolSelectConfigException($where . '：' . $k . ' 必須是 Hex 顏色，例 "#7A0000"');
                    }
                    $colors[$k] = $c;
                }
            }

            $href = trim((string) array_get($v, 'href', ''));
            if ($href !== '' && !self::validHref($href)) {
                throw new ToolSelectConfigException($where . '：href 只能是 "?m=..."、"/..." 或 http(s):// 開頭');
            }

            $dec = trim((string) array_get($v, 'decimals', ''));
            $key = trim((string) array_get($v, 'key', ''));
            $items[] = array(
                'id'       => $id,
                'category' => $cat,
                'label'    => trim((string) array_get($v, 'label', '')) !== '' ? trim((string) $v['label']) : $id,
                'key'      => $key !== '' ? $key : $id,
                'unit'     => trim((string) array_get($v, 'unit', '')),
                'decimals' => $dec === '' ? null : max(0, min(8, (int) $dec)),
                'spec'     => $spec,
                'toolSpec' => $toolSpec,
                'colors'   => $colors,
                'href'     => $href,
                '_cat'     => array_search($cat, array_keys($categories), true),
                '_order'   => $order++,
            );
        }

        // 依分類順序排，同分類保留 ini 順序（usort 不穩定，所以比兩個欄位）
        usort($items, function ($a, $b) {
            if ($a['_cat'] !== $b['_cat']) {
                return $a['_cat'] < $b['_cat'] ? -1 : 1;
            }
            return $a['_order'] < $b['_order'] ? -1 : 1;
        });
        foreach ($items as $i => $it) {
            unset($items[$i]['_cat'], $items[$i]['_order']);
        }

        self::$models[$model] = array(
            'model'      => $model,
            'label'      => $shared['models'][$model],
            'json_path'  => trim((string) array_get($page, 'json_path', '')),
            'sample'     => (bool) array_get($page, 'sample', false),
            'tools'      => $tools,
            'categories' => $categories,
            'items'      => $items,
        );
        return self::$models[$model];
    }

    /** 該項目在某台機台的有效 spec */
    public static function specOf($item, $tool)
    {
        return isset($item['toolSpec'][$tool]) ? $item['toolSpec'][$tool] : $item['spec'];
    }

    /**
     * 超規判定（剛好等於界限算正常）
     * @param float|null $v
     * @param array $s array(upper_oos, upper_ooc, lower_ooc, lower_oos)，null = 不判斷
     */
    public static function judge($v, $s)
    {
        if ($v === null) {
            return '';
        }
        if (($s[0] !== null && $v > $s[0]) || ($s[3] !== null && $v < $s[3])) {
            return 'oos';
        }
        if (($s[1] !== null && $v > $s[1]) || ($s[2] !== null && $v < $s[2])) {
            return 'ooc';
        }
        return '';
    }

    /** href：?m=...、/...、http(s)://...（不接受 //host 與 javascript: 等） */
    public static function validHref($href)
    {
        if (preg_match('/[\s"\'<>\\\\]/', $href)) {
            return false;
        }
        return (bool) preg_match('#^(\?|/(?!/)|https?://)#i', $href);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private static function parse($file, $label)
    {
        if (!is_file($file)) {
            throw new ToolSelectConfigException('找不到設定檔 ' . $label);
        }
        $ini = @parse_ini_file($file, true);
        if ($ini === false) {
            throw new ToolSelectConfigException('INI 解析失敗：' . $label . '（字串請加雙引號）');
        }
        return $ini;
    }

    private static function number($value, $where)
    {
        $value = trim((string) $value);
        if ($value === '') {
            return null;
        }
        if (!is_numeric($value)) {
            throw new ToolSelectConfigException($where . ' 必須是數字或留空（目前是「' . $value . '」）');
        }
        return (float) $value;
    }

    /** lower_oos <= lower_ooc < upper_ooc <= upper_oos（只比有填的） */
    private static function checkOrder($s, $where)
    {
        list($uoos, $uooc, $looc, $loos) = $s;
        $bad = ($loos !== null && $looc !== null && $loos > $looc)
            || ($looc !== null && $uooc !== null && $looc >= $uooc)
            || ($uooc !== null && $uoos !== null && $uooc > $uoos)
            || ($loos !== null && $uoos !== null && $loos >= $uoos)
            || ($loos !== null && $uooc !== null && $loos >= $uooc)
            || ($looc !== null && $uoos !== null && $looc >= $uoos);
        if ($bad) {
            throw new ToolSelectConfigException($where . '：spec 必須符合 lower_oos <= lower_ooc < upper_ooc <= upper_oos');
        }
    }

    private static function color($value, $default)
    {
        $value = trim((string) $value);
        return preg_match('/^#([0-9A-Fa-f]{3}|[0-9A-Fa-f]{6})$/', $value) ? strtoupper($value) : $default;
    }
}
#@@@ FILE modules/tool_select/ValueSource.php sha1=4f41777d8ae7faea09adbbba9f90637fc65f2153 base=- mode=644 enc=text nl=1
<?php
/**
 * 選機表數值：讀 config/tool_select/<機型>.ini [page] json_path 指定的 JSON 檔
 *
 * 格式（機台 → 項目 → 值）：
 *   {"updated": "2026-11-15 08:00",
 *    "data": {"APAN01": {"M1BF_Ry": 3.21, "LUSU": "Down", "SAMOS": {"v": -3.4, "t": "2026-11-15 07:30"}}}}
 *   也接受省略 "data" 這一層：{"APAN01": {...}, ...}
 *
 * - 不匯入資料庫；檔案修改時間或大小改變才重新解析，解析結果快取在 storage/cache/
 * - 標準 JSON 讀不到時，嘗試把 Python dict 印法（單引號、True/False/None）轉成 JSON 再讀
 *   （轉換函式沿用 tool_board 的 ToolBoardPm::pythonToJson）
 * - 機台代號一律大寫比對；項目名稱不分大小寫比對
 */
require_once AISLE_MODULES . '/tool_board/PmSource.php';

class ToolSelectValues
{
    const CACHE_VERSION = 1;
    const MAX_TEXT = 32;

    /**
     * @return array array(
     *   'status'  => 'off' | 'ok' | 'missing' | 'error' | 'sample',
     *   'message' => 提示訊息,
     *   'updated' => 資料時間（JSON 的 updated，沒有則為檔案修改時間）,
     *   'values'  => array(項目 id => array(機台 => array('v' => 數字|null, 's' => 狀態|null, 't' => 時間|null))))
     */
    public static function forModel($cfg)
    {
        if ($cfg['sample']) {
            return self::sample($cfg);
        }
        $all = self::load($cfg['json_path']);
        $values = array();
        if ($all['status'] === 'ok') {
            foreach ($cfg['items'] as $it) {
                $key = strtolower($it['key']);
                foreach ($cfg['tools'] as $tool) {
                    if (isset($all['data'][$tool][$key])) {
                        $values[$it['id']][$tool] = $all['data'][$tool][$key];
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return array(
            'status'  => $all['status'],
            'message' => $all['message'],
            'updated' => $all['updated'],
            'values'  => $values,
        );
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private static function load($path)
    {
        $base = array('status' => 'ok', 'message' => '', 'updated' => '', 'data' => array());
        if ($path === '') {
            $base['status'] = 'off';
            $base['message'] = '尚未設定數值 JSON 檔（機型設定檔 [page] json_path）';
            return $base;
        }
        clearstatcache(true, $path);
        if (!is_file($path)) {
            $base['status'] = 'missing';
            $base['message'] = '找不到數值檔：' . $path;
            return $base;
        }
        if (!is_readable($path)) {
            $base['status'] = 'error';
            $base['message'] = '數值檔無法讀取（權限）：' . $path;
            return $base;
        }
        $sig = filemtime($path) . '-' . filesize($path);

        $cacheFile = AISLE_STORAGE . '/cache/tool_select_' . md5($path) . '.json';
        if (is_file($cacheFile)) {
            $cached = json_decode((string) @file_get_contents($cacheFile), true);
            if (is_array($cached) && array_get($cached, 'v') === self::CACHE_VERSION && array_get($cached, 'sig') === $sig) {
                return $cached['data'];
            }
        }

        $data = self::parseFile($path, $base);
        if ($data['updated'] === '') {
            $data['updated'] = date('Y-m-d H:i:s', filemtime($path));
        }
        $dir = dirname($cacheFile);
        if (is_dir($dir) && is_writable($dir)) {
            $tmp = $cacheFile . '.' . getmypid() . '.tmp';
            if (@file_put_contents($tmp, json_encode(array('v' => self::CACHE_VERSION, 'sig' => $sig, 'data' => $data))) !== false) {
                @rename($tmp, $cacheFile);
            }
        }
        return $data;
    }

    private static function parseFile($path, $out)
    {
        $text = (string) file_get_contents($path);
        if (substr($text, 0, 3) === "\xEF\xBB\xBF") {
            $text = substr($text, 3);
        }
        $json = json_decode($text, true);
        if (!is_array($json)) {
            $json = json_decode(ToolBoardPm::pythonToJson($text), true);
            if (!is_array($json)) {
                $out['status'] = 'error';
                $out['message'] = '數值檔格式錯誤，無法解析（請確認產生端使用 json.dump 輸出）';
                return $out;
            }
            $out['message'] = '數值檔不是標準 JSON（單引號），已自動轉換；請產生端改用 json.dump 輸出';
        }

        if (isset($json['updated']) && is_string($json['updated'])) {
            $out['updated'] = self::text($json['updated']);
        }
        $tools = (isset($json['data']) && is_array($json['data'])) ? $json['data'] : $json;
        foreach ($tools as $tool => $items) {
            if (!is_array($items) || $tool === 'updated') {
                continue;
            }
            $toolId = strtoupper(trim((string) $tool));
            foreach ($items as $key => $raw) {
                $cell = self::cell($raw);
                if ($cell !== null) {
                    $out['data'][$toolId][strtolower(trim((string) $key))] = $cell;
                }
            }
        }
        return $out;
    }

    /** 一格的值 → array('v','s','t')；沒有值回傳 null */
    private static function cell($raw)
    {
        $t = null;
        if (is_array($raw)) {
            if (isset($raw['t']) && is_scalar($raw['t'])) {
                $t = self::text($raw['t']);
            }
            $raw = array_key_exists('v', $raw) ? $raw['v'] : null;
        }
        if ($raw === null || is_array($raw) || is_bool($raw)) {
            return null;
        }
        if (is_int($raw) || is_float($raw)) {
            return array('v' => (float) $raw, 's' => null, 't' => $t);
        }
        $s = trim((string) $raw);
        if ($s === '') {
            return null;
        }
        if (is_numeric($s)) {
            return array('v' => (float) $s, 's' => null, 't' => $t);
        }
        return array('v' => null, 's' => self::text($s), 't' => $t);
    }

    /** 文字：最多 32 個字（以 UTF-8 字元為單位，不依賴 mbstring） */
    private static function text($v)
    {
        $v = trim((string) $v);
        return preg_match('/^.{0,' . self::MAX_TEXT . '}/us', $v, $m) ? $m[0] : substr($v, 0, self::MAX_TEXT);
    }

    /** 範例值：依機台 + 項目產生固定的數值（每次相同），大部分在規格內，少數超出 OOC / OOS */
    private static function sample($cfg)
    {
        $values = array();
        foreach ($cfg['items'] as $it) {
            foreach ($cfg['tools'] as $tool) {
                $h = abs(crc32($tool . '|' . $it['id']));
                $s = ToolSelectConfig::specOf($it, $tool);
                $hi = $s[0] !== null ? $s[0] : ($s[1] !== null ? $s[1] * 1.1 : 10);
                $lo = $s[3] !== null ? $s[3] : ($s[2] !== null ? $s[2] * 1.1 : 0);
                if ($s[3] === null && $s[2] === null) {
                    $lo = $hi > 0 ? 0 : $hi * 2;
                }
                $r = ($h % 1000) / 1000;          // 0 ~ 1
                if ($h % 23 === 0) {
                    $values[$it['id']][$tool] = array('v' => null, 's' => 'Down', 't' => null);
                    continue;
                }
                if ($h % 17 === 0) {
                    continue;                      // 沒有資料
                }
                $span = $hi - $lo;
                $v = $lo + $span * (0.05 + $r * 1.0);   // 少數會超出上限
                if ($h % 11 === 0) {
                    $v = $lo - $span * 0.06;            // 少數低於下限
                }
                $values[$it['id']][$tool] = array('v' => round($v, 3), 's' => null, 't' => date('Y-m-d') . ' 08:00');
            }
        }
        return array(
            'status'  => 'sample',
            'message' => '目前使用範例值（機型設定檔 [page] sample = On）。準備好 JSON 檔後填 json_path 並把 sample 改成 Off。',
            'updated' => date('Y-m-d') . ' 08:00',
            'values'  => $values,
        );
    }
}
#@@@ FILE modules/tool_select/controller.php sha1=78d2defffc56316623690080783dbd27396a71ea base=- mode=644 enc=text nl=1
<?php
/**
 * 設備選機表（Tool Selection Matrix）
 *
 * 共用設定 config/tool_select.ini；機型專屬 config/tool_select/<機型>.ini（機台、分類、項目、spec、JSON 路徑）
 * 頁面唯讀；匯出 CSV 由 config/config.ini 的 [page.tool_select] action.export 決定哪些身份可用
 *
 *   index    頁面（表格、篩選都由 tool_select.js 處理）
 *   data     GET  該機型：機台、分類、項目（含每台有效 spec）、數值、資料時間
 *   export   GET  CSV：目前篩選的項目 × 機台（items=id,id&tools=T1,T2；不給 = 全部）
 */
require_once dirname(__FILE__) . '/SelectConfig.php';
require_once dirname(__FILE__) . '/ValueSource.php';

class ToolSelectController extends Controller
{
    public function indexAction()
    {
        $error = '';
        $models = array();
        try {
            foreach (ToolSelectConfig::models() as $m => $label) {
                $modelError = '';
                try {
                    ToolSelectConfig::forModel($m);
                } catch (ToolSelectConfigException $e) {
                    $modelError = $e->getMessage();
                }
                $models[] = array('key' => $m, 'label' => $label, 'error' => $modelError);
            }
        } catch (ToolSelectConfigException $e) {
            $error = $e->getMessage();
        }

        $this->render('index', array(
            'error'  => $error,
            'models' => $models,
            'config' => array(
                'urls'      => array(
                    'data'   => url($this->module, 'data'),
                    'export' => url($this->module, 'export'),
                    'base'   => base_url() . '/index.php',
                ),
                'models'    => $models,
                'model'     => strtoupper($this->query('model')),
                'canExport' => $this->can('export'),
            ),
        ));
    }

    public function dataAction()
    {
        $cfg = $this->modelConfig();
        if ($cfg === null) {
            return;
        }
        $shared = ToolSelectConfig::shared();
        $vals = ToolSelectValues::forModel($cfg);

        $categories = array();
        foreach ($cfg['categories'] as $code => $label) {
            $categories[] = array('code' => $code, 'label' => $label);
        }
        $items = array();
        foreach ($cfg['items'] as $it) {
            $items[] = array(
                'id'       => $it['id'],
                'category' => $it['category'],
                'label'    => $it['label'],
                'unit'     => $it['unit'],
                'decimals' => $it['decimals'],
                'spec'     => $it['spec'],
                'toolSpec' => (object) $it['toolSpec'],
                'colors'   => (object) $it['colors'],
                'href'     => $it['href'],
            );
        }

        $this->json(array(
            'ok'         => true,
            'model'      => $cfg['model'],
            'label'      => $cfg['label'],
            'tools'      => $cfg['tools'],
            'categories' => $categories,
            'items'      => $items,
            'values'     => (object) $vals['values'],
            'status'     => $vals['status'],
            'message'    => $vals['message'],
            'updated'    => $vals['updated'],
            'colors'     => $shared['colors'],
            'statusColors' => (object) $shared['status'],
            'serverTime' => date('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
        ));
    }

    public function exportAction()
    {
        $cfg = $this->modelConfig();
        if ($cfg === null) {
            return;
        }
        $vals = ToolSelectValues::forModel($cfg);
        $values = $vals['values'];

        $tools = $this->pick($this->query('tools'), $cfg['tools'], true);
        $ids = array();
        foreach ($cfg['items'] as $it) {
            $ids[] = $it['id'];
        }
        $wanted = array_flip($this->pick($this->query('items'), $ids, false));

        $filename = 'tool_select_' . $cfg['model'] . '_' . date('Ymd_Hi') . '.csv';
        header('Content-Type: text/csv; charset=utf-8');
        header('Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="' . $filename . '"');
        header('Cache-Control: private, no-store');
        $out = fopen('php://output', 'w');
        fwrite($out, "\xEF\xBB\xBF"); // UTF-8 BOM，Excel 直接開不會亂碼
        fputcsv($out, array_merge(array('分類', '項目', 'uOOS', 'uOOC', 'lOOC', 'lOOS'), $tools, array('OOC 機台', 'OOS 機台')));
        foreach ($cfg['items'] as $it) {
            if (!isset($wanted[$it['id']])) {
                continue;
            }
            $label = $it['label'] . ($it['unit'] !== '' ? ' (' . $it['unit'] . ')' : '');
            $line = array(self::safe($cfg['categories'][$it['category']]), self::safe($label));
            foreach ($it['spec'] as $s) {
                $line[] = $s === null ? '' : self::num($s, null);
            }
            $ooc = array();
            $oos = array();
            foreach ($tools as $tool) {
                $cell = isset($values[$it['id']][$tool]) ? $values[$it['id']][$tool] : null;
                if ($cell === null) {
                    $line[] = '';
                    continue;
                }
                if ($cell['v'] === null) {
                    $line[] = self::safe($cell['s']);
                    continue;
                }
                $line[] = self::num($cell['v'], $it['decimals']);
                $j = ToolSelectConfig::judge($cell['v'], ToolSelectConfig::specOf($it, $tool));
                if ($j === 'oos') {
                    $oos[] = $tool;
                } elseif ($j === 'ooc') {
                    $ooc[] = $tool;
                }
            }
            $line[] = implode(' ', $ooc);
            $line[] = implode(' ', $oos);
            fputcsv($out, $line);
        }
        fclose($out);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------

    /** 讀 ?model= 對應的機型設定；錯誤時已輸出 JSON 並回傳 null */
    private function modelConfig()
    {
        $model = strtoupper($this->query('model'));
        try {
            $models = ToolSelectConfig::models();
            if (!isset($models[$model])) {
                $this->json(array('ok' => false, 'error' => '沒有這個機型：' . $model), 404);
                return null;
            }
            return ToolSelectConfig::forModel($model);
        } catch (ToolSelectConfigException $e) {
            $this->json(array('ok' => false, 'error' => $e->getMessage()), 500);
            return null;
        }
    }

    /** 逗號分隔的清單 → 只留 $all 裡有的，依 $all 的順序；空白 = 全部 */
    private function pick($param, $all, $upper)
    {
        if ($param === '') {
            return $all;
        }
        $want = array();
        foreach (explode(',', $param) as $v) {
            $v = trim($v);
            $want[$upper ? strtoupper($v) : $v] = true;
        }
        $out = array();
        foreach ($all as $v) {
            if (isset($want[$v])) {
                $out[] = $v;
            }
        }
        return $out;
    }

    private static function num($v, $decimals)
    {
        if ($decimals !== null) {
            return number_format($v, $decimals, '.', '');
        }
        // 去掉浮點數尾巴（0.1 + 0.2 之類），最多 10 位有效小數
        return rtrim(rtrim(sprintf('%.10F', $v), '0'), '.');
    }

    /** 避免 Excel 把 = + - @ 開頭的文字當公式執行 */
    private static function safe($s)
    {
        $s = (string) $s;
        return ($s !== '' && strpos('=+-@', $s[0]) !== false) ? "'" . $s : $s;
    }
}
#@@@ FILE modules/tool_select/views/index.php sha1=3d34371ae76aa8d344c2d8771c75151a2237801f base=- mode=644 enc=text nl=1
<?php

#@@@ FILE public/assets/js/charts.js sha1=15c6153fea6ba260ec858f0f5c952eb8860efd2d base=b882857db11fc4ea4943b6747918ce3449b092f9 mode=644 enc=text nl=1
/*
 * charts 頁面 (jQuery 1.12 + ECharts 5)，所有 chart（BSLr、M1BF…）共用
 *
 * 三個區塊：single 單台趨勢 / newest 最新值排排站 / tbt 歷史趨勢排排站
 * 每個區塊依 charts/<名稱>.ini 的 [plot.xxx] 各畫一張圖（CFG.groups），
 * 同一組的參數（例 M1BF_Z_C1、M1BF_Z_C2）畫在同一張圖上。
 *
 * 後端回傳格式：
 *   { "APAN01": { "Date": [...], "Data": { "M1BF_Z_C1": [...], "M1BF_Z_C2": [...] } }, ... }
 *   每個參數陣列與 Date 等長，沒有值的位置是 null
 *   選用 "Url": [...]（與 Date 等長）：單台趨勢圖點擊數據點時在新分頁開啟該網址
 *
 * 資料載入方式：
 *   進入頁面時只呼叫一次 tbt，取回日期區間內「全部機台、全部參數」的資料存在 cache，所有圖都從這份資料取得：
 *     TBT 直接畫；single 取出所選機台；Newest_TBT 取每台每個參數在區間內的最後一個值（再依機型篩選）
 *   之後切換機台、切換機型只用 cache 重畫，不再發 request；
 *   只有改日期區間（查詢 / 快速區間）才會重新取 tbt。
 *
 * 編碼方式：顏色 = 機台，線型 / 長條紋理 = 參數（同組第 1 個參數實線無紋理，第 2 個虛線斜紋…）
 * 只用 ES5 語法，避免舊瀏覽器出錯
 */
(function ($, window) {
    'use strict';

    var CFG = window.AISLE_CHARTS;
    if (!$ || !CFG) {
        return;
    }

    // ---------- 主題 ----------
    // 機台顏色：8 色類別色盤（已用 dataviz validate_palette.js 檢查深色底 #001406：
    // 亮度、彩度、色盲區分、對比全部通過）。同機型內依序取色，APAN01 與 APAP01 同色，
    // 以 X 軸機台名稱區分。顏色跟著機台走，篩選後不會變色。
    var PALETTE = ['#3987e5', '#d95926', '#199e70', '#c98500', '#d55181', '#008300', '#9085e9', '#e66767'];
    // 參數：線型與長條紋理，依參數在組內的順序取用
    var LINE_TYPES = ['solid', 'dashed', 'dotted', [10, 4, 2, 4], [2, 6]];
    var LINE_NAMES = ['實線', '虛線', '點線', '點劃線', '稀點線'];
    var DECALS = [
        null,
        { symbol: 'rect', symbolSize: 1, dashArrayX: [1, 0], dashArrayY: [2, 4], rotation: Math.PI / 4, color: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)' },
        { symbol: 'circle', symbolSize: 0.6, dashArrayX: [[4, 4]], dashArrayY: [4, 0], color: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)' },
        { symbol: 'rect', symbolSize: 1, dashArrayX: [1, 0], dashArrayY: [2, 4], rotation: -Math.PI / 4, color: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)' },
        { symbol: 'rect', symbolSize: 1, dashArrayX: [1, 0], dashArrayY: [1, 3], rotation: 0, color: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)' }
    ];
    // 文字、格線、軸線等依畫面風格切換（body.theme-light = 淺色）；機台色盤兩種風格共用
    // （PALETTE 另以淺色底 #ffffff 檢查過，同樣全部通過）
    var THEMES = {
        dark: {
            INK: '#00ff41',                 // 主要文字
            INK_DIM: '#00b82e',             // 次要文字
            GRID: '#0a2a12',                // 格線
            AXIS: '#00661a',                // 軸線
            NEUTRAL: '#7dffb0',             // 圖例中代表「參數」的中性色
            BAND: 'rgba(0, 255, 65, 0.05)', // TBT 交錯底色
            INACTIVE: '#1f4a2a',            // 圖例關閉的項目
            TIP_BG: 'rgba(0, 10, 3, 0.95)',
            TIP_SHADOW: 'box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(0,255,65,0.3);',
            MASK: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6)',
            HOVER: 'rgba(0,255,65,0.06)',
            ZOOM_FILL: 'rgba(0,255,65,0.12)',
            ZOOM_AREA: 'rgba(0,255,65,0.08)',
            OOS: '#ff3b3b',                 // spec 線：OOS（規格界限）
            OOC: '#ffb000'                  // spec 線：OOC（管制界限）
        },
        light: {
            INK: '#00591a',
            INK_DIM: '#2e6b3c',
            GRID: '#dcefe1',
            AXIS: '#8fbf9a',
            NEUTRAL: '#2e6b3c',
            BAND: 'rgba(0, 143, 17, 0.06)',
            INACTIVE: '#b5cdb9',
            TIP_BG: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.97)',
            TIP_SHADOW: 'box-shadow: 0 2px 10px rgba(0,80,20,0.18);',
            MASK: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7)',
            HOVER: 'rgba(0,143,17,0.08)',
            ZOOM_FILL: 'rgba(0,143,17,0.15)',
            ZOOM_AREA: 'rgba(0,143,17,0.10)',
            OOS: '#c62828',
            OOC: '#b26a00'
        }
    };
    var T, INK, INK_DIM, GRID, AXIS, NEUTRAL, BAND;
    function setTheme(name) {
        T = THEMES[name] || THEMES.dark;
        INK = T.INK;
        INK_DIM = T.INK_DIM;
        GRID = T.GRID;
        AXIS = T.AXIS;
        NEUTRAL = T.NEUTRAL;
        BAND = T.BAND;
    }
    setTheme($('body').hasClass('theme-light') ? 'light' : 'dark');
    // spec 線的顏色依風格取 T.OOS / T.OOC，畫圖時才查（切換風格後會跟著變）
    var SPEC_KEYS = [
        { key: 'upper_oos', label: 'OOS', colorKey: 'OOS', type: 'solid' },
        { key: 'upper_ooc', label: 'OOC', colorKey: 'OOC', type: 'dashed' },
        { key: 'lower_ooc', label: 'OOC', colorKey: 'OOC', type: 'dashed' },
        { key: 'lower_oos', label: 'OOS', colorKey: 'OOS', type: 'solid' }
    ];
    var FONT = '"Share Tech Mono", Consolas, "Courier New", "Microsoft JhengHei", monospace';

    var COLOR = {};
    (function assignColors() {
        var counter = {};
        $.each(CFG.tools, function (i, tool) {
            var model = CFG.models[tool] || '';
            counter[model] = counter[model] || 0;
            COLOR[tool] = PALETTE[counter[model] % PALETTE.length];
            counter[model]++;
        });
    })();

    var GROUPS = {};
    $.each(CFG.groups, function (i, g) { GROUPS[g.id] = g; });

    function colorOf(tool) { return COLOR[tool] || NEUTRAL; }
    function lineTypeOf(i) { return LINE_TYPES[i % LINE_TYPES.length]; }
    function decalOf(i) { return DECALS[i % DECALS.length]; }
    function shortName(param, group) {
        // 圖例只顯示參數名稱與組標題不同的部分，例 M1BF_Z 組內的 M1BF_Z_C1 → C1
        var prefix = group.title + '_';
        return param.indexOf(prefix) === 0 && param.length > prefix.length ? param.substring(prefix.length) : param;
    }

    function baseOption() {
        return {
            backgroundColor: 'transparent',
            textStyle: { color: INK, fontFamily: FONT },
            animationDuration: 400,
            tooltip: {
                backgroundColor: T.TIP_BG,
                borderColor: INK_DIM,
                borderWidth: 1,
                textStyle: { color: INK, fontFamily: FONT, fontSize: 13 },
                extraCssText: T.TIP_SHADOW
            },
            grid: { left: 50, right: 24, top: 40, bottom: 40, containLabel: false }
        };
    }

    /** Y 軸；config 有設上下限就固定，否則自動（自動時會把 spec 線納入範圍）。kind = single / newest / tbt */
    function valueAxis(group, kind) {
        var lim = (group.limits && group.limits[kind]) || {};
        var axis = {
            type: 'value',
            name: group.unit || 'Data',
            nameTextStyle: { color: INK_DIM },
            axisLine: { show: false },
            axisTick: { show: false },
            axisLabel: { color: INK_DIM },
            splitLine: { lineStyle: { color: GRID } },
            scale: kind !== 'newest'
        };
        var spec = specValues(group);
        if (lim.min !== null && lim.min !== undefined) {
            axis.min = lim.min;
        } else if (spec.length) {
            axis.min = function (v) { return specBounds(v, spec, kind).min; };
        }
        if (lim.max !== null && lim.max !== undefined) {
            axis.max = lim.max;
        } else if (spec.length) {
            axis.max = function (v) { return specBounds(v, spec, kind).max; };
        }
        return axis;
    }

    /** 這組圖有設定的 spec 值（數字陣列） */
    function specValues(group) {
        var out = [];
        $.each(SPEC_KEYS, function (i, k) {
            var v = group.spec ? group.spec[k.key] : null;
            if (v !== null && v !== undefined) {
                out.push(v);
            }
        });
        return out;
    }

    /** 資料範圍 v（{min, max}）加上 spec 線後的 Y 軸上下限，取整到刻度間距 */
    function specBounds(v, spec, kind) {
        var lo = Math.min.apply(null, spec);
        var hi = Math.max.apply(null, spec);
        if (isFinite(v.min)) {
            lo = Math.min(lo, v.min);
        }
        if (isFinite(v.max)) {
            hi = Math.max(hi, v.max);
        }
        if (kind === 'newest') {   // 長條圖從 0 開始
            lo = Math.min(lo, 0);
            hi = Math.max(hi, 0);
        }
        var span = hi - lo;
        if (span <= 0) {
            span = Math.abs(hi) || 1;
        }
        var step = niceStep(span / 5);
        var min = Math.floor(lo / step) * step;
        var max = Math.ceil(hi / step) * step;
        return { min: +min.toFixed(10), max: +max.toFixed(10) };
    }

    function niceStep(x) {
        var p = Math.pow(10, Math.floor(Math.log(x) / Math.LN10));
        var f = x / p;
        return (f <= 1 ? 1 : f <= 2 ? 2 : f <= 5 ? 5 : 10) * p;
    }

    /** spec 水平線：放在一條沒有資料的輔助 series 上；沒設定 spec 時回傳 null */
    function specSeries(group) {
        var lines = [];
        $.each(SPEC_KEYS, function (i, k) {
            var v = group.spec ? group.spec[k.key] : null;
            if (v === null || v === undefined) {
                return;
            }
            lines.push({
                name: k.key,
                yAxis: v,
                lineStyle: { color: T[k.colorKey], type: k.type, width: 1.5 },
                label: {
                    show: true,
                    position: k.key.indexOf('upper') === 0 ? 'insideEndTop' : 'insideEndBottom',
                    color: T[k.colorKey],
                    fontFamily: FONT,
                    formatter: k.label + ' ' + fmt(v)
                }
            });
        });
        if (!lines.length) {
            return null;
        }
        return {
            name: '_spec',
            type: 'line',
            data: [],
            silent: true,
            markLine: { silent: true, symbol: 'none', animation: false, data: lines }
        };
    }

    /** 有 spec 時把輔助 series 加到 series 陣列最後 */
    function pushSpec(series, group) {
        var s = specSeries(group);
        if (s) {
            series.push(s);
        }
    }

    function axisLine() {
        return { lineStyle: { color: AXIS } };
    }

    function legendBase(data) {
        return {
            type: 'scroll',
            top: 0,
            data: data,
            textStyle: { color: INK, fontFamily: FONT },
            pageTextStyle: { color: INK },
            pageIconColor: INK,
            pageIconInactiveColor: AXIS,
            inactiveColor: T.INACTIVE,
            itemWidth: 26,
            itemHeight: 10
        };
    }

    function fmt(v) {
        return v === null || v === undefined ? '-' : AISLE.escape(v);
    }

    // ---------- 圖表盒 ----------
    // boxes['tbt:m1bf_z'] = { kind, group, $el, chart, shown }
    var boxes = {};
    var cache = { tbt: null };
    var request = null;

    function eachBox(kind, fn) {
        $.each(boxes, function (key, box) {
            if (!kind || box.kind === kind) {
                fn(box);
            }
        });
    }

    function showMessage(chart, text) {
        chart.clear();
        chart.setOption({
            title: {
                text: text,
                left: 'center',
                top: 'middle',
                textStyle: { color: INK_DIM, fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 'normal', fontFamily: FONT }
            }
        });
    }

    function loadingOpts() {
        return { text: 'LOADING...', color: INK, textColor: INK, maskColor: T.MASK, fontFamily: FONT };
    }

    /** 把資料畫到圖表盒；沒有資料時顯示 NO DATA，並更新資料表 */
    function show(box, data, draw) {
        box.shown = data;
        box.urlInfo = null;
        if ($.isEmptyObject(data)) {
            showMessage(box.chart, '> 查無資料 // NO DATA');
        } else {
            draw(box, data);
        }
        renderTable(box);
    }

    /** 資料表檢視：機台 / 時間 / 該組每個參數一欄 */
    function renderTable(box) {
        var params = box.group.params;
        var head = '<tr><th>TOOL</th><th>DATE</th>';
        $.each(params, function (i, p) { head += '<th>' + AISLE.escape(p) + '</th>'; });
        head += '</tr>';
        var rows = [];
        var withUrl = false;
        $.each(box.shown, function (tool, s) {
            if ($.isArray(s.Url)) {
                withUrl = true;
            }
        });
        if (withUrl) {
            head = head.replace(/<\/tr>$/, '<th>URL</th></tr>');
        }
        $.each(box.shown, function (tool, s) {
            if (!$.isArray(s.Date)) {
                // newest：各參數的日期分開存，值後面附上日期
                var tr0 = '<tr><td>' + AISLE.escape(tool) + '</td><td>LATEST</td>';
                $.each(params, function (j, p) {
                    tr0 += '<td>' + fmt(s.Data[p][0]) + (s.Date[p][0] ? ' <span class="dim">(' + AISLE.escape(s.Date[p][0]) + ')</span>' : '') + '</td>';
                });
                rows.push(tr0 + '</tr>');
                return;
            }
            for (var i = 0; i < s.Date.length; i++) {
                var tr = '<tr><td>' + AISLE.escape(tool) + '</td><td>' + AISLE.escape(s.Date[i]) + '</td>';
                for (var j = 0; j < params.length; j++) {
                    tr += '<td>' + fmt(s.Data[params[j]] ? s.Data[params[j]][i] : null) + '</td>';
                }
                if (withUrl) {
                    var u = $.isArray(s.Url) ? safeUrl(s.Url[i]) : null;
                    tr += '<td>' + (u ? '<a href="' + AISLE.escape(u) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">開啟</a>' : '-') + '</td>';
                }
                rows.push(tr + '</tr>');
            }
        });
        box.$el.find('.cd-table').html(rows.length
            ? '<table class="table"><thead>' + head + '</thead><tbody>' + rows.join('') + '</tbody></table>'
            : '<p class="dim">&gt; 查無資料</p>');
    }

    // ---------- 1. single：所選機台，組內每個參數一條線 ----------
    function drawSingle(box, data) {
        var group = box.group;
        var tool;
        for (tool in data) {
            if (data.hasOwnProperty(tool)) {
                break;
            }
        }
        var s = data[tool];
        var urls = $.isArray(s.Url) ? s.Url : null;
        var series = [];
        $.each(group.params, function (pi, param) {
            var points = [];
            var col = s.Data[param] || [];
            for (var i = 0; i < s.Date.length; i++) {
                points.push([s.Date[i], col[i] === undefined ? null : col[i]]);
            }
            series.push({
                name: param,
                type: 'line',
                data: points,
                connectNulls: !!group.connect,   // ini 的 connect = On：跳過空值把線連起來
                showSymbol: points.length <= 45,
                symbolSize: 8,
                color: colorOf(tool),
                lineStyle: { width: 2, type: lineTypeOf(pi) },
                emphasis: { focus: 'series' }
            });
        });
        var opt = baseOption();
        opt.legend = legendBase(group.params.slice(0));
        opt.legend.formatter = function (name) { return shortName(name, group); };
        opt.tooltip.trigger = 'axis';
        opt.tooltip.axisPointer = { type: 'line', lineStyle: { color: INK_DIM, type: 'dashed' } };
        // tooltip 標題一律顯示完整時間（後端已統一成 Y-m-d H:i:s）；有 Url 時多一行提示，點數據點開新分頁（見 bindUrlClick）
        opt.tooltip.formatter = function (params) {
            var html = '';
            var idx = null;
            $.each(params, function (i, p) {
                if (p.seriesName === '_spec') {
                    return;
                }
                if (!html) {
                    html = AISLE.escape(s.Date[p.dataIndex]);
                    idx = p.dataIndex;
                }
                html += '<br>' + p.marker + AISLE.escape(p.seriesName) + '：<b>' + fmt(p.value[1]) + '</b>';
            });
            if (urls && idx !== null && safeUrl(urls[idx])) {
                html += '<br><span style="color:' + INK_DIM + '">&gt; 點擊數據點開啟連結</span>';
            }
            return html;
        };
        if (urls) {
            box.urlInfo = { dates: s.Date, urls: urls, cols: $.map(group.params, function (p) { return [s.Data[p] || []]; }) };
        }
        opt.xAxis = {
            type: 'time',
            axisLine: axisLine(),
            axisLabel: { color: INK_DIM, hideOverlap: true, formatter: '{yyyy}-{MM}-{dd} {HH}:{mm}:{ss}' },
            splitLine: { show: false }
        };
        opt.yAxis = valueAxis(group, 'single');
        opt.dataZoom = [{ type: 'inside' }];
        pushSpec(series, group);
        opt.series = series;
        box.chart.clear();
        box.chart.setOption(opt);
    }

    // ---------- 2. newest：X 軸機台，組內每個參數一根長條 ----------
    function drawNewest(box, data) {
        var group = box.group;
        var tools = [];
        $.each(data, function (tool) { tools.push(tool); });
        var series = [];
        $.each(group.params, function (pi, param) {
            var bars = [];
            $.each(tools, function (ti, tool) {
                var s = data[tool];
                var style = { color: colorOf(tool), borderRadius: [4, 4, 0, 0] };
                if (decalOf(pi)) {
                    style.decal = decalOf(pi);
                }
                bars.push({ value: s.Data[param][0], date: s.Date[param][0], itemStyle: style });
            });
            // series 的 itemStyle 只決定圖例的樣子（中性色 + 該參數的紋理），每根長條另用機台色
            var legendStyle = { color: NEUTRAL };
            if (decalOf(pi)) {
                legendStyle.decal = decalOf(pi);
            }
            series.push({
                name: param,
                type: 'bar',
                data: bars,
                barMaxWidth: 28,
                barGap: '15%',
                itemStyle: legendStyle,
                emphasis: { focus: 'series', itemStyle: { shadowBlur: 12, shadowColor: INK } },
                cursor: 'pointer'
            });
        });
        var opt = baseOption();
        opt.legend = legendBase(group.params.slice(0));
        opt.legend.formatter = function (name) { return shortName(name, group); };
        opt.tooltip.trigger = 'axis';
        opt.tooltip.axisPointer = { type: 'shadow', shadowStyle: { color: T.HOVER } };
        opt.tooltip.formatter = function (params) {
            if (!params.length) {
                return '';
            }
            var html = '<b>' + AISLE.escape(params[0].name) + '</b>';
            $.each(params, function (i, p) {
                html += '<br>' + p.marker + AISLE.escape(p.seriesName) + '：<b>' + fmt(p.value) + '</b>' +
                    ' <span style="color:' + INK_DIM + '">(' + fmt(p.data.date) + ')</span>';
            });
            return html;
        };
        opt.xAxis = {
            type: 'category',
            data: tools,
            axisLine: axisLine(),
            axisTick: { show: false },
            axisLabel: { color: INK, interval: 0, rotate: tools.length > 14 ? 45 : 0 }
        };
        opt.yAxis = valueAxis(group, 'newest');
        pushSpec(series, group);
        opt.series = series;
        box.chart.clear();
        box.chart.setOption(opt);
    }

    // ---------- 3. TBT ----------
    // X 軸為類別軸：每台機台佔一段，段內依時間排列。每台每個參數一條 series，
    // 同一台的 series 用同一個名稱，所以圖例以機台為單位開關；其他段落填 null，
    // 所以每台只畫在自己的區段，相鄰機台以交錯底色分隔。
    function drawTbt(box, data) {
        var group = box.group;
        var keys = [];      // 類別值（唯一）："APAN01|2026-01-01"
        var segments = [];  // { tool, from, to }
        $.each(data, function (tool, s) {
            var from = keys.length;
            for (var i = 0; i < s.Date.length; i++) {
                keys.push(tool + '|' + s.Date[i]);
            }
            segments.push({ tool: tool, from: from, to: keys.length - 1 });
        });

        var centers = {};
        var bands = [];
        $.each(segments, function (i, seg) {
            centers[Math.floor((seg.from + seg.to) / 2)] = seg.tool;
            if (i % 2 === 1) {
                bands.push([{ xAxis: keys[seg.from] }, { xAxis: keys[seg.to] }]);
            }
        });

        var series = [];
        var legend = [];
        var paramOf = [];   // seriesIndex → 參數名稱（tooltip 用）
        $.each(segments, function (i, seg) {
            var s = data[seg.tool];
            legend.push(seg.tool);
            $.each(group.params, function (pi, param) {
                var col = s.Data[param] || [];
                var arr = new Array(keys.length);
                for (var k = 0; k < keys.length; k++) {
                    arr[k] = null;
                }
                for (var j = 0; j < s.Date.length; j++) {
                    arr[seg.from + j] = col[j] === undefined ? null : col[j];
                }
                paramOf.push(param);
                series.push({
                    name: seg.tool,
                    type: 'line',
                    data: arr,
                    connectNulls: !!group.connect,   // ini 的 connect = On：跳過空值把線連起來
                    showSymbol: false,
                    symbolSize: 8,
                    color: colorOf(seg.tool),
                    lineStyle: { width: 2, type: lineTypeOf(pi) },
                    emphasis: { focus: 'series' }
                });
            });
        });
        // 交錯底色放在一條不顯示的輔助 series 上
        series.push({
            name: '_bands',
            type: 'line',
            data: [],
            silent: true,
            markArea: { silent: true, itemStyle: { color: BAND }, data: bands }
        });

        // 機台名稱轉 90 度：X 軸下方的高度依最長的名稱計算（名稱 + 刻度間距 + 縮放拉桿），
        // 圖表高度同步加高，資料區不會因為名稱變長而被壓縮
        var labelPx = 0;
        $.each(segments, function (i, seg) { labelPx = Math.max(labelPx, textWidth(seg.tool)); });
        var bottom = Math.max(70, Math.ceil(labelPx) + 52);
        fitHeight(box, bottom - 70);

        var opt = baseOption();
        opt.grid.top = 64;
        opt.grid.bottom = bottom;
        opt.legend = legendBase(legend);
        opt.tooltip.trigger = 'axis';
        opt.tooltip.axisPointer = { type: 'line', lineStyle: { color: INK_DIM, type: 'dashed' } };
        opt.tooltip.formatter = function (params) {
            var html = '';
            $.each(params, function (i, p) {
                if (p.seriesName === '_bands' || p.seriesName === '_spec' || p.value === null || p.value === undefined) {
                    return;
                }
                if (!html) {
                    html = p.marker + AISLE.escape(p.seriesName) + '<br>' + AISLE.escape(String(p.name).split('|')[1]);
                }
                html += '<br>' + AISLE.escape(paramOf[p.seriesIndex]) + '：<b>' + fmt(p.value) + '</b>';
            });
            return html;
        };
        opt.xAxis = {
            type: 'category',
            data: keys,
            boundaryGap: false,
            axisLine: axisLine(),
            axisTick: { show: false },
            axisLabel: {
                color: INK,
                rotate: 90,
                interval: function (idx) { return centers.hasOwnProperty(idx); },
                formatter: function (v) { return String(v).split('|')[0]; }
            },
            splitLine: { show: false }
        };
        opt.yAxis = valueAxis(group, 'tbt');
        opt.dataZoom = [
            { type: 'inside' },
            {
                type: 'slider', height: 18, bottom: 12,
                borderColor: AXIS, fillerColor: T.ZOOM_FILL,
                handleStyle: { color: INK_DIM }, moveHandleStyle: { color: INK_DIM },
                dataBackground: { lineStyle: { color: AXIS }, areaStyle: { color: T.ZOOM_AREA } },
                textStyle: { color: INK_DIM },
                labelFormatter: function (i, v) { return String(v).replace('|', ' '); }
            }
        ];
        pushSpec(series, group);
        opt.series = series;
        box.chart.clear();
        box.chart.setOption(opt);

        var styles = $.map(group.params, function (p, i) {
            return shortName(p, group) + ' ' + LINE_NAMES[i % LINE_NAMES.length];
        });
        box.$el.find('.cd-box-info').text(segments.length + ' 台 · ' + keys.length + ' 個時間點 · ' + styles.join(' / '));
    }

    /** 文字在圖表字型下的寬度（px），用來計算轉 90 度後需要的高度 */
    var measureCtx = null;
    function textWidth(text) {
        if (measureCtx === null) {
            var c = document.createElement('canvas');
            measureCtx = c.getContext ? c.getContext('2d') : false;
        }
        if (!measureCtx) {
            return String(text).length * 8;
        }
        measureCtx.font = '12px ' + FONT;
        return measureCtx.measureText(String(text)).width;
    }

    /** 圖表高度 = CSS 原本的高度 + extra（px）；高度有變時通知 ECharts 重新排版 */
    function fitHeight(box, extra) {
        var $c = box.$el.find('.cd-chart');
        var before = $c.height();
        $c.css('height', '');                       // 先還原，讀 CSS（含手機版）的原始高度
        var h = $c.height() + Math.max(0, extra);
        $c.height(h);
        if (h !== before) {
            box.chart.resize();
        }
    }

    // ---------- single 點擊數據點開啟 Url ----------
    /** 只接受 http://、https:// 或 / 開頭（不含 //）的網址，其他回傳 null（後端已過濾，這裡再擋一次） */
    function safeUrl(u) {
        if (typeof u !== 'string') {
            return null;
        }
        u = $.trim(u);
        return /^https?:\/\/\S+$/i.test(u) || /^\/(?!\/)\S*$/.test(u) ? u : null;
    }

    /** 滑鼠位置最近、且有網址的數據點（距離 14px 內），回傳網址或 null */
    function urlAt(box, x, y) {
        var info = box.urlInfo;
        if (!info || !box.chart.containPixel('grid', [x, y])) {
            return null;
        }
        var best = null;
        var bestDist = 14 * 14;
        for (var i = 0; i < info.dates.length; i++) {
            var u = safeUrl(info.urls[i]);
            if (!u) {
                continue;
            }
            for (var c = 0; c < info.cols.length; c++) {
                var v = info.cols[c][i];
                if (v === null || v === undefined) {
                    continue;
                }
                var pt = box.chart.convertToPixel({ xAxisIndex: 0, yAxisIndex: 0 }, [info.dates[i], v]);
                if (!pt) {
                    continue;
                }
                var d = (pt[0] - x) * (pt[0] - x) + (pt[1] - y) * (pt[1] - y);
                if (d <= bestDist) {
                    bestDist = d;
                    best = u;
                }
            }
        }
        return best;
    }

    /** single 圖：點擊數據點在新分頁開啟網址；滑過有網址的點顯示手指游標。沒有 Url 時不做任何事 */
    function bindUrlClick(box) {
        var zr = box.chart.getZr();
        zr.on('mousemove', function (e) {
            if (box.urlInfo && urlAt(box, e.offsetX, e.offsetY)) {
                zr.setCursorStyle('pointer');
            }
        });
        zr.on('click', function (e) {
            var u = urlAt(box, e.offsetX, e.offsetY);
            if (u) {
                var w = window.open(u, '_blank');
                if (w) {
                    w.opener = null;
                }
            }
        });
    }

    // ---------- 從 cache 取出各圖要的資料 ----------
    /** 只保留某組的參數（有 Url 時一併帶上） */
    function pick(s, params) {
        var out = { Date: s.Date, Data: {} };
        $.each(params, function (i, p) { out.Data[p] = s.Data[p] || []; });
        if ($.isArray(s.Url)) {
            out.Url = s.Url;
        }
        return out;
    }

    function showTbt() {
        eachBox('tbt', function (box) {
            var data = {};
            $.each(cache.tbt, function (tool, s) { data[tool] = pick(s, box.group.params); });
            show(box, data, drawTbt);
        });
    }

    /** 單台機台：從 cache.tbt 取出，不發 request */
    function showSingle() {
        var tool = $('#cd-tool').val();
        $('.cd-single-title').text(tool ? '· ' + tool : '');
        if (!cache.tbt || !tool) {
            return;
        }
        eachBox('single', function (box) {
            var data = {};
            if (cache.tbt[tool]) {
                data[tool] = pick(cache.tbt[tool], box.group.params);
            }
            show(box, data, drawSingle);
        });
    }

    /**
     * 最新值：每台每個參數在區間內最後一個非 null 的值，再依機型篩選，不發 request
     * 各參數最後一筆的日期可能不同，所以 Date 也依參數分開存：
     *   { Date: { param: [日期] }, Data: { param: [值] } }
     */
    function showNewest() {
        if (!cache.tbt) {
            return;
        }
        var model = $('#cd-model').val() || 'All';  // ini 沒設定機型時沒有下拉選單
        eachBox('newest', function (box) {
            var data = {};
            $.each(cache.tbt, function (tool, s) {
                if (model !== 'All' && CFG.models[tool] !== model) {
                    return;
                }
                var latest = { Date: {}, Data: {} };
                var any = false;
                $.each(box.group.params, function (pi, p) {
                    var col = s.Data[p] || [];
                    latest.Date[p] = [null];
                    latest.Data[p] = [null];
                    for (var i = col.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                        if (col[i] !== null && col[i] !== undefined) {
                            latest.Date[p] = [s.Date[i]];
                            latest.Data[p] = [col[i]];
                            any = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                });
                if (any) {
                    data[tool] = latest;
                }
            });
            show(box, data, drawNewest);
        });
    }

    // ---------- 選擇器 ----------
    function pad(n) { return n < 10 ? '0' + n : String(n); }
    function ymd(d) { return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate()); }
    function isDate(s) { return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s); }

    function range() {
        var start = $.trim($('#cd-start').val());
        var end = $.trim($('#cd-end').val());
        var $msg = $('#cd-msg').text('');
        if (!isDate(start) || !isDate(end)) {
            $msg.text('> 日期格式需為 YYYY-MM-DD');
            return null;
        }
        if (start > end) {
            $msg.text('> 起始日期不可晚於結束日期');
            return null;
        }
        return { start: start, end: end };
    }

    /** 取日期區間內全部機台、全部參數的資料（所有圖共用），進頁面與改日期時呼叫 */
    function loadRange() {
        var r = range();
        if (!r) {
            return;
        }
        if (request) {
            request.abort();
        }
        eachBox(null, function (box) { box.chart.showLoading('default', loadingOpts()); });
        request = $.ajax({ url: CFG.urls.tbt, data: { start: r.start, end: r.end }, dataType: 'json', cache: false })
            .done(function (res) {
                cache.tbt = res || {};
                eachBox(null, function (box) { box.chart.hideLoading(); });
                showTbt();
                showSingle();
                showNewest();
            })
            .fail(function (xhr, status) {
                if (status === 'abort') {
                    return;
                }
                var msg = (xhr.responseJSON && xhr.responseJSON.error) || ('HTTP ' + xhr.status);
                eachBox(null, function (box) {
                    box.chart.hideLoading();
                    showMessage(box.chart, '> ERROR: ' + msg);
                });
            });
    }

    $(function () {
        if (!window.echarts) {
            $('.cd-chart').html('<p class="text-red">&gt; ECharts 載入失敗：請確認可連到 CDN，或執行 scripts/shell/fetch_vendor.sh 放到 public/assets/vendor/</p>');
            return;
        }
        $('.cd-box').each(function () {
            var $el = $(this);
            var kind = $el.data('kind');
            var group = GROUPS[$el.data('group')];
            boxes[kind + ':' + group.id] = {
                kind: kind,
                group: group,
                $el: $el,
                chart: window.echarts.init($el.find('.cd-chart')[0]),
                shown: {},
                urlInfo: null
            };
            if (kind === 'single') {
                bindUrlClick(boxes[kind + ':' + group.id]);
            }
        });

        if (!CFG.tools.length) {
            eachBox(null, function (box) { showMessage(box.chart, '> 沒有任何機台 // NO TOOLS'); });
            return;
        }

        $('#cd-filters').on('submit', function () {
            loadRange();
            return false;
        });
        $('#cd-tool').on('change', showSingle);
        $('#cd-model').on('change', showNewest);
        $('.cd-quick').on('click', 'button', function () {
            var days = parseInt($(this).data('days'), 10);
            var end = new Date();
            var start = new Date(end.getTime() - (days - 1) * 86400000);
            $('#cd-start').val(ymd(start));
            $('#cd-end').val(ymd(end));
            loadRange();
        });

        // 點最新值長條 → 切換單台機台
        eachBox('newest', function (box) {
            box.chart.on('click', function (p) {
                if (p.componentType === 'series' && p.name) {
                    $('#cd-tool').val(p.name);
                    showSingle();
                    var $first = $('.cd-box[data-kind="single"]').first();
                    if ($first.length) {
                        $('html, body').animate({ scrollTop: $first.offset().top - 80 }, 200);
                    }
                }
            });
        });

        function boxOf(el) {
            var $el = $(el).closest('.cd-box');
            return boxes[$el.data('kind') + ':' + $el.data('group')];
        }
        $('.cd-legend-all').on('click', function () {
            boxOf(this).chart.dispatchAction({ type: 'legendAllSelect' });
        });
        $('.cd-legend-none').on('click', function () {
            var box = boxOf(this);
            $.each(box.shown, function (tool) {
                box.chart.dispatchAction({ type: 'legendUnSelect', name: tool });
            });
        });
        $('.cd-table-btn').on('click', function () {
            boxOf(this).$el.find('.cd-table').toggle();
        });

        // 切換畫面風格：換配色後用 cache 重畫，不重新取資料
        $(document).on('aisle:theme', function (e, theme) {
            setTheme(theme);
            if (cache.tbt) {
                showTbt();
                showSingle();
                showNewest();
            }
        });

        var resizeTimer = null;
        $(window).on('resize', function () {
            clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
            resizeTimer = setTimeout(function () {
                eachBox(null, function (box) { box.chart.resize(); });
            }, 150);
        });

        loadRange();

        // 網址帶 &tool=（例如從選機表點數值格過來）：捲到單台機台趨勢
        if (CFG.focusTool) {
            var $single = $('.cd-box[data-kind="single"]').first();
            if ($single.length) {
                $('html, body').scrollTop($single.offset().top - 80);
            }
        }
    });
})(window.jQuery, window);
#@@@ FILE public/assets/js/tool_select.js sha1=58b53df61fcdf7709fc06d6c90f3fde4ebe17cc7 base=- mode=644 enc=text nl=1
/*
 * AISLE 設備選機表（jQuery 1.12，ES5 寫法）
 *   - 機型頁籤：每個機型各自的機台、分類、項目、spec、數值
 *   - 項目篩選（列）：分類多選下拉 + 關鍵字；機台篩選（欄）：可搜尋的多選下拉
 *   - 數值超出 OOC / OOS 依設定上底色；項目名稱 / 數值格可連到 chart（href 的 {tool} 換成機台）
 *   - 篩選全部在瀏覽器端進行，條件依機型記在 localStorage
 *   - 全螢幕：標題列按鈕或快捷鍵 F；Esc 恢復（瀏覽器不允許全螢幕時改成鋪滿瀏覽器視窗）
 * 設定由 view 放在 window.TOOL_SELECT
 */
(function ($) {
    if (!$ || !window.TOOL_SELECT) {
        return;
    }
    var CFG = window.TOOL_SELECT;
    var MODEL_KEY = 'aisle.tool_select.model';
    var FILTER_KEY = 'aisle.tool_select.filter.';
    var SPEC_NAMES = ['uOOS', 'uOOC', 'lOOC', 'lOOS'];

    var state = {
        model: '',
        data: null,
        request: null,
        // 存「被取消勾選的」分類 / 機台，設定檔之後新增的分類、機台預設會顯示
        hiddenCats: {},
        hiddenTools: {},
        q: '',
        oocOnly: false
    };
    var catDd = null, toolDd = null;

    // ==================================================================
    // 小工具
    // ==================================================================
    function esc(s) {
        return String(s === null || s === undefined ? '' : s)
            .replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
            .replace(/"/g, '&quot;').replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
    }
    function store(key, value) {
        try {
            if (value === undefined) {
                return window.localStorage.getItem(key);
            }
            window.localStorage.setItem(key, value);
        } catch (e) { /* 私密模式等：不記憶 */ }
        return null;
    }
    function isLight() { return $('body').hasClass('theme-light'); }
    function keys(obj) {
        var out = [];
        $.each(obj, function (k, v) { if (v) { out.push(k); } });
        return out;
    }
    function toSet(list) {
        var o = {};
        $.each(list || [], function (i, v) { o[v] = true; });
        return o;
    }

    /** 數字顯示：有 decimals 就固定位數，否則去掉浮點數尾巴 */
    function fmt(v, decimals) {
        if (v === null || v === undefined) {
            return '-';
        }
        if (decimals !== null && decimals !== undefined) {
            return v.toFixed(decimals);
        }
        return String(parseFloat(v.toPrecision(10)));
    }
    function specText(v) { return v === null || v === undefined ? '-' : String(parseFloat(v.toPrecision(10))); }

    /** 剛好等於界限算正常 */
    function judge(v, s) {
        if (v === null || v === undefined) {
            return '';
        }
        if ((s[0] !== null && v > s[0]) || (s[3] !== null && v < s[3])) {
            return 'oos';
        }
        if ((s[1] !== null && v > s[1]) || (s[2] !== null && v < s[2])) {
            return 'ooc';
        }
        return '';
    }
    function specOf(item, tool) {
        return item.toolSpec && item.toolSpec[tool] ? item.toolSpec[tool] : item.spec;
    }

    /** 依底色亮度決定文字用黑或白 */
    function textOn(hex) {
        var h = hex.replace('#', '');
        if (h.length === 3) {
            h = h.charAt(0) + h.charAt(0) + h.charAt(1) + h.charAt(1) + h.charAt(2) + h.charAt(2);
        }
        var r = parseInt(h.substr(0, 2), 16), g = parseInt(h.substr(2, 2), 16), b = parseInt(h.substr(4, 2), 16);
        return (r * 299 + g * 587 + b * 114) / 1000 > 140 ? '#000000' : '#FFFFFF';
    }
    function bgOf(item, level) {
        var k = level + '_bg' + (isLight() ? '_light' : '');
        return (item.colors && item.colors[k]) || state.data.colors[k];
    }

    /** href：{tool} 換成機台（點項目名稱時為空白）；?m=... 接上網站路徑前綴 */
    function hrefOf(item, tool) {
        if (!item.href) {
            return '';
        }
        var h = item.href.replace(/\{tool\}/g, tool ? encodeURIComponent(tool) : '');
        return h.charAt(0) === '?' ? CFG.urls.base + h : h;
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // 多選下拉（分類、機台共用）
    // ==================================================================
    /**
     * opts: { search: bool, noun: '台', placeholder, onChange(hiddenSet) }
     * setOptions(list[{value,label}], hiddenSet)
     */
    function MultiDropdown($root, opts) {
        var self = this;
        this.$root = $root;
        this.opts = opts;
        this.list = [];
        this.hidden = {};
        $root.html(
            '<button type="button" class="ts-dd-btn"><span class="ts-dd-text"></span> <span class="ts-dd-caret">▾</span></button>' +
            '<div class="ts-dd-panel" style="display:none">' +
            (opts.search ? '<input type="text" class="ts-dd-search" placeholder="' + esc(opts.placeholder || '搜尋') + '" autocomplete="off">' : '') +
            '<div class="ts-dd-actions">' +
            '<button type="button" class="btn btn-sm" data-act="all">全選</button>' +
            '<button type="button" class="btn btn-sm" data-act="none">全不選</button>' +
            '<button type="button" class="btn btn-sm" data-act="invert">反選</button>' +
            '</div><div class="ts-dd-list"></div></div>'
        );
        this.$btn = $root.find('.ts-dd-btn');
        this.$panel = $root.find('.ts-dd-panel');
        this.$list = $root.find('.ts-dd-list');
        this.$search = $root.find('.ts-dd-search');

        this.$btn.on('click', function () {
            var open = self.$panel.is(':visible');
            $('.ts-dd-panel').hide();
            if (!open) {
                self.$panel.show();
                self.$search.val('');
                self.filterList();
                self.$search.focus();
            }
        });
        this.$search.on('input keyup', function () { self.filterList(); });
        this.$list.on('change', 'input', function () {
            self.hidden[this.value] = !this.checked;
            self.changed();
        });
        $root.find('.ts-dd-actions').on('click', 'button', function () {
            var act = $(this).data('act');
            // 搜尋中只作用在看得到的項目
            self.$list.find('label:visible input').each(function () {
                var on = act === 'all' ? true : (act === 'none' ? false : !this.checked);
                this.checked = on;
                self.hidden[this.value] = !on;
            });
            self.changed();
        });
    }
    MultiDropdown.prototype.setOptions = function (list, hidden) {
        var html = '';
        this.list = list;
        this.hidden = {};
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
            var v = list[i].value;
            if (hidden[v]) {
                this.hidden[v] = true;
            }
            html += '<label class="ts-dd-item" data-text="' + esc(String(list[i].label).toLowerCase()) + '">' +
                '<input type="checkbox" value="' + esc(v) + '"' + (hidden[v] ? '' : ' checked') + '> ' + esc(list[i].label) + '</label>';
        }
        this.$list.html(html || '<div class="dim">（無）</div>');
        this.updateText();
    };
    MultiDropdown.prototype.selected = function () {
        var out = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < this.list.length; i++) {
            if (!this.hidden[this.list[i].value]) {
                out.push(this.list[i]);
            }
        }
        return out;
    };
    MultiDropdown.prototype.filterList = function () {
        var q = $.trim(this.$search.val() || '').toLowerCase();
        this.$list.find('label').each(function () {
            $(this).toggle(q === '' || $(this).attr('data-text').indexOf(q) >= 0);
        });
    };
    MultiDropdown.prototype.updateText = function () {
        var sel = this.selected(), n = this.list.length, text;
        if (sel.length === n) {
            text = '全部' + (n ? ' ' + n + ' ' + this.opts.noun : '');
        } else if (!sel.length) {
            text = '未選擇';
        } else if (sel.length <= 2) {
            text = $.map(sel, function (o) { return o.label; }).join(', ');
        } else {
            text = sel[0].label + ', ' + sel[1].label + ' +' + (sel.length - 2) + '（' + sel.length + ' / ' + n + '）';
        }
        this.$btn.find('.ts-dd-text').text(text);
        this.$btn.toggleClass('is-filtered', sel.length !== n);
    };
    MultiDropdown.prototype.changed = function () {
        this.updateText();
        this.opts.onChange(this.hidden);
    };

    // 點下拉以外的地方收起（Esc 在下面的全螢幕區塊一起處理）
    $(document).on('mousedown', function (e) {
        if (!$(e.target).closest('.ts-dd').length) {
            $('.ts-dd-panel').hide();
        }
    });

    // ==================================================================
    // 全螢幕 / 恢復（做法同借機看板）
    //   優先用瀏覽器全螢幕 API；瀏覽器不允許時改成「頁面內最大化」（蓋滿瀏覽器視窗）
    //   Esc：先收起開著的下拉選單，再按一次離開全螢幕
    // ==================================================================
    var FS_TEXT = { off: '[ ⛶ 全螢幕 ]', on: '[ 🗗 恢復 ]' };
    function rootEl() { return document.getElementById('ts-root'); }
    function fsElement() {
        return document.fullscreenElement || document.webkitFullscreenElement || document.msFullscreenElement || null;
    }
    function isFull() { return $('#ts-root').hasClass('ts-full'); }
    function fitWrap() {
        var $wrap = $('#ts-wrap');
        if (isFull()) {
            var top = $wrap.offset().top - $(window).scrollTop();
            $wrap.css('max-height', Math.max(200, $(window).height() - top - 16) + 'px');
        } else {
            $wrap.css('max-height', '');
        }
    }
    function setFull(on, nativeFs) {
        $('#ts-root').toggleClass('ts-full', on).toggleClass('ts-full-native', on && !!nativeFs);
        $('body').toggleClass('ts-body-full', on);
        $('#ts-fs').text(on ? FS_TEXT.on : FS_TEXT.off).attr('title', on ? '恢復正常大小（Esc）' : '選機表全螢幕（快捷鍵 F）');
        setTimeout(fitWrap, 0);
    }
    function enterFull() {
        var el = rootEl();
        var req = el.requestFullscreen || el.webkitRequestFullscreen || el.msRequestFullscreen;
        if (!req) {
            setFull(true, false);
            return;
        }
        try {
            var p = req.call(el);
            if (p && p.then) {
                p.then(null, function () { setFull(true, false); });   // 被瀏覽器或政策擋下：頁面內最大化
            }
        } catch (err) {
            setFull(true, false);
        }
    }
    function exitFull() {
        if (fsElement()) {
            var ex = document.exitFullscreen || document.webkitExitFullscreen || document.msExitFullscreen;
            if (ex) {
                ex.call(document);
                return;
            }
        }
        setFull(false);
    }
    function toggleFull() {
        if (isFull()) {
            exitFull();
        } else {
            enterFull();
        }
    }
    $(document).on('fullscreenchange webkitfullscreenchange MSFullscreenChange', function () {
        var el = fsElement();
        if (el && el === rootEl()) {
            setFull(true, true);
        } else if (!el && $('#ts-root').hasClass('ts-full-native')) {
            setFull(false);
        }
    });
    $(document).on('keydown', function (e) {
        if (e.ctrlKey || e.altKey || e.metaKey) {
            return;
        }
        if (e.which === 27) {
            // 先收起下拉選單；沒有開著的下拉選單時離開全螢幕
            // （瀏覽器全螢幕時多數瀏覽器自己會處理 Esc，這裡再補一次，收不到也沒關係）
            if ($('.ts-dd-panel:visible').length) {
                $('.ts-dd-panel').hide();
            } else if (isFull()) {
                exitFull();
            }
            return;
        }
        if ($(e.target).is('input, textarea, select')) {
            return;
        }
        if ((e.key === 'f' || e.key === 'F' || e.which === 70) && !e.shiftKey) {
            e.preventDefault();
            toggleFull();
        }
    });
    $(window).on('resize', fitWrap);

    // ==================================================================
    // 篩選條件記憶
    // ==================================================================
    function loadFilter(model) {
        var f = null;
        try {
            f = JSON.parse(store(FILTER_KEY + model) || 'null');
        } catch (e) { f = null; }
        f = f || {};
        state.hiddenCats = toSet(f.hiddenCats);
        state.hiddenTools = toSet(f.hiddenTools);
        state.q = typeof f.q === 'string' ? f.q : '';
        state.oocOnly = !!f.oocOnly;
    }
    function saveFilter() {
        if (!state.model) {
            return;
        }
        store(FILTER_KEY + state.model, JSON.stringify({
            hiddenCats: keys(state.hiddenCats),
            hiddenTools: keys(state.hiddenTools),
            q: state.q,
            oocOnly: state.oocOnly
        }));
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // 載入
    // ==================================================================
    function selectModel(model) {
        var ok = false;
        $.each(CFG.models, function (i, m) { if (m.key === model) { ok = true; } });
        if (!ok) {
            model = CFG.models.length ? CFG.models[0].key : '';
        }
        if (!model) {
            $('#ts-wrap').html('<div class="dim ts-empty">&gt; 沒有任何機型</div>');
            return;
        }
        state.model = model;
        store(MODEL_KEY, model);
        $('#ts-tabs .pi-tab').removeClass('active').filter('[data-model="' + model + '"]').addClass('active');
        if (window.history && window.history.replaceState) {
            var url = window.location.href.replace(/([?&])model=[^&#]*(&)?/, function (all, p, amp) { return amp ? p : ''; })
                .replace(/[?&]$/, '').replace(/#.*$/, '');
            window.history.replaceState(null, '', url + (url.indexOf('?') >= 0 ? '&' : '?') + 'model=' + encodeURIComponent(model));
        }
        loadFilter(model);
        $('#ts-q').val(state.q);
        $('#ts-ooc-only').prop('checked', state.oocOnly);
        load();
    }

    function load() {
        if (state.request) {
            state.request.abort();
        }
        $('#ts-error').hide();
        $('#ts-refresh').prop('disabled', true);
        state.request = $.ajax({ url: CFG.urls.data, data: { model: state.model }, dataType: 'json', cache: false })
            .done(function (res) {
                state.data = res;
                $('#ts-title').text('TOOL SELECT // ' + res.label);
                $('#ts-updated').text(res.updated ? '資料時間 ' + res.updated : '');
                if (res.message) {
                    $('#ts-msg').text('> ' + res.message).show();
                } else {
                    $('#ts-msg').hide();
                }
                catDd.setOptions($.map(res.categories, function (c) { return { value: c.code, label: c.label }; }), state.hiddenCats);
                toolDd.setOptions($.map(res.tools, function (t) { return { value: t, label: t }; }), state.hiddenTools);
                state.hiddenCats = $.extend({}, catDd.hidden);
                state.hiddenTools = $.extend({}, toolDd.hidden);
                renderLegend();
                render();
            })
            .fail(function (xhr, status) {
                if (status === 'abort') {
                    return;
                }
                state.data = null;
                var msg = (xhr.responseJSON && xhr.responseJSON.error) || ('HTTP ' + xhr.status);
                $('#ts-error').text('> ' + msg).show();
                $('#ts-msg').hide();
                $('#ts-wrap').html('');
                $('#ts-summary').text('');
            })
            .always(function () {
                $('#ts-refresh').prop('disabled', false);
            });
    }

    // ==================================================================
    // 畫表格
    // ==================================================================
    function visibleTools() {
        var out = [];
        $.each(state.data.tools, function (i, t) {
            if (!state.hiddenTools[t]) {
                out.push(t);
            }
        });
        return out;
    }

    function visibleItems(tools) {
        var d = state.data;
        var catLabel = {};
        $.each(d.categories, function (i, c) { catLabel[c.code] = c.label; });
        var words = $.grep($.trim(state.q).toLowerCase().split(/\s+/), function (w) { return w !== ''; });
        return $.grep(d.items, function (it) {
            if (state.hiddenCats[it.category]) {
                return false;
            }
            if (words.length) {
                var text = (catLabel[it.category] + ' ' + it.label).toLowerCase();
                var hit = false;
                for (var i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
                    if (text.indexOf(words[i]) >= 0) {
                        hit = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (!hit) {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            if (state.oocOnly) {
                var vals = d.values[it.id] || {};
                for (var j = 0; j < tools.length; j++) {
                    var c = vals[tools[j]];
                    if (c && judge(c.v, specOf(it, tools[j])) !== '') {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
                return false;
            }
            return true;
        });
    }

    function cellHtml(it, tool, c) {
        var s = specOf(it, tool);
        var ovr = !!(it.toolSpec && it.toolSpec[tool]);
        var cls = ['ts-v'], style = '', text = '-', level = '';
        var tip = [tool + ' · ' + it.label];
        if (!c) {
            cls.push('ts-na');
            tip.push('沒有資料');
        } else if (c.v === null) {
            text = c.s;
            var sc = state.data.statusColors[String(c.s).toLowerCase()];
            if (sc) {
                style = 'background:' + sc + ';color:' + textOn(sc);
            }
            cls.push('ts-status');
            tip.push('狀態：' + c.s);
        } else {
            text = fmt(c.v, it.decimals);
            level = judge(c.v, s);
            tip.push('值：' + text + (it.unit ? ' ' + it.unit : '') + '　判定：' + (level === 'oos' ? 'OOS' : (level === 'ooc' ? 'OOC' : '正常')));
            if (level) {
                var bg = bgOf(it, level);
                style = 'background:' + bg + ';color:' + textOn(bg);
                cls.push('ts-' + level);
            }
        }
        if (c && c.t) {
            tip.push('資料時間：' + c.t);
        }
        if (ovr) {
            cls.push('ts-ovr');
            tip.push('此機台 spec：uOOS ' + specText(s[0]) + ' / uOOC ' + specText(s[1]) +
                ' / lOOC ' + specText(s[2]) + ' / lOOS ' + specText(s[3]));
        }
        var href = c ? hrefOf(it, tool) : '';
        var inner = esc(text);
        if (href) {
            tip.push(it.href.indexOf('{tool}') >= 0 ? '點擊開啟 ' + tool + ' 的 chart' : '點擊開啟 chart');
            inner = '<a href="' + esc(href) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">' + inner + '</a>';
        }
        return '<td class="' + cls.join(' ') + '"' + (style ? ' style="' + style + '"' : '') +
            ' title="' + esc(tip.join('\n')) + '">' + inner + '</td>';
    }

    function render() {
        var d = state.data;
        if (!d) {
            return;
        }
        var tools = visibleTools();
        var items = visibleItems(tools);
        $('#ts-summary').text('顯示 ' + items.length + ' / ' + d.items.length + ' 項、' + tools.length + ' / ' + d.tools.length + ' 台');
        $('#ts-clear').toggleClass('is-active', !!(keys(state.hiddenCats).length || keys(state.hiddenTools).length || state.q || state.oocOnly));

        if (!d.items.length) {
